a format-7 document refused by name and its match voided at the boot). `MatchRecord.minds` (per unit, the sortie id and the whole `MindSeal`) joins the live record under `#[serde(default)]` with `LIVE_FORMAT` 1 unmoved and nothing in the result document. **The pilot's sentence** (§5, the M1b.4 and Q.2 entries): a destroyed turret is told `The turret is destroyed: its weapons are gone; its core is gone.` on a line tank and `…; its core is degraded.` on the heavy, the debrief's `your turret destroyed: its weapons are gone; its core is gone.`, each core clause only where true of the tank now (never on a hull never fitted with a core, never for a degrade at the floor); the bot's reader still matches `its weapons are gone`; no `FRAME_VERSION` move, no keyframe field.
- **§3.1 "Durability" and the AF.3 entry (two more resume refusals), §3.8 (two more check-out refusals), §4 (four ledger kinds and the source store), §3.7 (the third controller's dormant row and the `Released` hand-back), §6 (the deliberation layer and a dormant row's order), §7 (`[tacsim.minds]`)**: as built (17b cuts M3.3, M3.4, M3.5 and M3.6, 2026-10-06 and 2026-10-07, `94021032`, `fa55ac17`, `fbc5124a`, `d8f5af94`; doc 17 §8, §9, §12, §13, D-UM-31, D-UM-32, D-UM-36, D-UM-45, D-UM-46 and §24): **every unit of every check-out is sealed** — `MindSeal` computed once and last in `arena_def::custody_unit` from the finished custody (the seat's pilot, the side lead or nobody as the principal; the Line card; the captured floor; `engine` `reflex` for a reflex AND a piloted hull, `native` for a Mind hull — superseded by 17b cut M4.3: every hull `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE`, `scripted` (*`native` since the fill decision of 2026-10-09, the M4.3 entry's next: `standard.rhai` the reference held equal by Gate A and the twin, run in production only by a pre-decision match's scripted seals* (*until 17b cut M4.6's format 9, which voids every older snapshot*)), `reflex` decode-only), with a sortie id `(match_id << 32) | unit` minted at the seal; **the tape**: every match now opens `match_started` then one `mind_sealed` per unit (the sortie, the mind, the source's first sixteen digits, the card digest, the class, the governed bit, the licence, the principal) and every sortie closes once with `sortie_closed { destroyed }` right after the wreck's `unit_destroyed` or `sortie_closed { match_end }` for each open sortie **immediately before the end's own line** at every one of the six end sites (the end stays the last line, W.2 kept; the ledger-full end and a void at boot close nothing), and `mind_fault { unit, think_seq, cause }` and `mind_dropped { unit, cause, fault, ops }` journal a mind's faults and its drop to the ROM (the cause word `strikes` or `core`, the last fault's word and the budget on the line so the bridge reads no live config, V.3's rule) — so the production `state_hash` of every match moved once, declared and re-blessed in M3.6 (the substep pins, the line pins compared ignoring `seq` and `hash`, `seq() == 1 + units` after `Arena::new`, `M2_CROSS_PLATFORM_PINS`, the harness baseline), no action hash being opened for a think; **the resume** (§3.1, the AF.3 entry's nineteen reasons now twenty-one): `ResumeRefusal::MindAbi` on every seal whose `abi` is not this build's `MIND_ABI` (so a deploy that bumps it voids every in-flight match at that boot, reflex-sealed ones included) and `MindSource` on a scripted seal whose source `tacsim:mindsrc:<sha256>` (written once at boot for the stock script, content-addressed, never rewritten) the store lacks, each voided by name through `void_at_boot` with no tape line; **check-out** (§3.8): `StartError::CardUnlawful` (`{callsign}'s card asks for odds under the Compact floor of {floor/10}%; the match cannot start.`; `[tacsim.minds].odds_floor_permille` now 10 to 950 in steps of 10, refuse-not-clamp) and `StartError::Pool { team, refusal }` as the last refusal before the first write (doc 17 §9's sentence; `[tacsim.minds].{pool_base_ops_s, pool_per_unit_ops_s, pool_floor_ops_s}` and `classes` the new config keys, every one with a stock default, commented in the deploy template), unable to refuse at the stock constants (twelve Class III a side is 216,000 against a half of 238,000 and `TooManyUnits` fires first); **the third controller** (§3.7, the M1a.5 entry): `set_controller(Mind → Pilot)` keeps the mind's row dormant (its notices and held order gated on the controller, the pilot's never entering the buffer) and a `Released` hands the hull back to `Controller::Mind` where a row stands, else to `Reflex` as before — no production hull has a row before M4, so the fill's hand-back and the production hash on that path are unchanged (superseded by 17b cut M4.3: every `Released` hands the hull to a mind, the row seeded `Withdraw`, and the dormant row's order is cleared at the possess); the one line a dropped core costs is told to the warrant principal alone (`Your core tripped: over its 1,500-op budget three thinks running. The hull is on the ROM; MIND is off for the match.` and its siblings; `RB's …` to the side lead for the fill; `Arena match {id}: {callsign} dropped to the ROM: …` to staff), every line under the 120-column floor and in the bot's contract as a neutral line; **§6**: a possessed hull's dormant row keeps a squad order its squad has since released (doc 17 §24; an M4 ruling). No verb, no draw label, no keyframe field, no proto, gateway binary or web change; `cargo test -p mush-bot` and the gateway twins green on the moved sentence.
- **§3.1 "The seam" and "Durability" (a mind's think is answered at the next substep's head; the arena task waits on a pool)**: as built (17b cut M4.1, 2026-10-07, `c9d3b7ae`; doc 17 §24 holds the detail): a mind's think is collected at its millisecond and its answer written at the head of the next substep — inline inside `Arena::step`, or by the arena task's delivery from a process-wide pool of `[tacsim.minds].workers` (2) threads, the batch submitted after `publish` and awaited at the next pulse's head and before a due snapshot is encoded, so a snapshot never carries an unanswered think (`SnapshotError::Awaiting`). The pool's wait is off the stall watchdog's clock; a failed batch, a stall or a runner panic voids the match. The per-substep hash sequence and the tape are the same on both paths, and a mind's fault lines carry the hash of the substep its think fell in (so the tape is not `t_ms`-monotonic across them). No format, frame or sentence moved.
- **§8 and D-16-15 (the harness: the minds lane, the arena lanes on minds, the baseline's host)**: as built (17b cut M4.2, 2026-10-08, `9be0b463`, and 17b cut M4.5b, 2026-10-09, `f19a88fb`; doc 17 §24 and 17b §2 hold the figures): one more `#[ignore]`d lane, `tacsim_harness_arena_minds`, fields the relief cohort as unit minds in four variants (the production fill, two all-ROM stock variants and a Class III ceiling) with a seeded quarter of wakes one substep late, a `held=`/`sound=` verdict per variant and a CPU lens computed from the host's own measured rate; since M4.5b every other lane fields its hulls as Class 0 minds on the native ROM, the report's `reflex` object is `motor` and its state rows `minds.states_at_end` (106 compared leaves where there were 109). The baseline is `crates/tacsim/tests/harness_baseline/arena.linux.json` — blessed once on Linux with the four minds blocks, `arena.windows.json` removed, then re-blessed on the conclav.io VPS by T-109 (its entry above) with the fill decision's leaves carried at the merge. The P3 gate's three weekly runs on that host are owed by the owner (17b §2).
- **§6 "The reflex controller" (the AI fill is a unit mind), §3.7 (`EXIT`, a drop, the third controller: a `Released` hands every hull to its mind), §3.1 "Pulse phases" step 5 (the production hash moved once), §3.6 and §5 (what a pilot reads is unchanged: the Y.4 sentence, the tempo, the leader's brief, the watch's `m`), §7 (`[tacsim.minds].fill_class`; `fill_controller` retired)**: as built (17b cut M4.3, 2026-10-08, `fbf23905`, the flip's commit — the sha written in by 17b cut M4.7's record, the entry having ridden the commit it describes; doc 17 §17's divergences written here as §17 asks, §22 M4, D-UM-37, D-UM-49; M4.2's decision `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE = scripted`, *amended 2026-10-09 by the fill decision, the next entry: `native`*). **The flip**: `match_::start` and `@arena/start` check every unseated hull out `Controller::Mind`, and EVERY hull — the fill's and a seated pilot's alike — is sealed with Standard Doctrine at `class = min(I, the core, [tacsim.minds].fill_class)` on `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE` (`scripted`: `standard.rhai` under `RhaiRunner`, installed per seal from the compiled-in source; *`native` since the fill decision, the next entry*), so a release hands any hull to a mind of the same class on the same engine; `fill_controller` is gone (a config still naming it refuses the boot with serde's `unknown field \`fill_controller\``), `fill_class` is `0`, `I`, `II` or `III` by word (default `I`; `0` fields every hull on the drive ROM; above `I` caps nothing until M5 rates minds (*corrected by 17b cut M5's record, C-21: until the cut that rates a mind above Class I; M5 fields other minds only through a test seam, and nobody authors one before doc 17's M6*)), and the bot rig's `arena_config_with_fill` is gone with it — the shipped fill IS the conditioned T1 the bot lane flies. **The pool at both gates**: the fill is counted at `fill_class` at `SIM UNIT` (the side's unclaimed pods under the AI fill ride `spoken`, and a choice that adds nothing is never refused, so `would think 0` is never said) and at check-out; twenty-four hulls at Class I speak for 36,000 of the 476,000 pool, 18,000 a side against a half of 238,000; a seated hull adds nothing at the pool (M5's gap, declared; *closed by 17b cut M5.1: every hull that chose no mind, seated or not, is admitted at `fill_class`, §11*) and is sealed at `fill_class` so a release adds no unadmitted load. The two call-offs the flip made reachable through the lobby read short: `Match 12 could not start: red's compute (144,000 ops a second) is over its half (138,000). The pods are free again.` (118 columns) and `Match 12 could not start: RC's sealed mind has no source on this server. The pods are free again.` (97; unreachable in production, the stock source being compiled in); every other refusal keeps its own sentence, and the staff path keeps the long `Display`. **`Released`**: every hull goes to a mind — the dormant row a possess left, or a fresh one — with its memory seeded `state = Withdraw, ordered = false` and the six Q.7/E.4 keys cleared exactly as `ReflexMemory::adopt` cleared them (`stand_until_ms`, `quiet_since_ms`, `rallied`, `last_plot`, `unheld_since_ms`, `hunt`; a memory of another shape becomes the stock seed), so N.1, Y.3 and Y.4 hold: the tank drives to its entry zone, rallies after `rally_ms` of quiet, and the Y.4 sentence `The machine was withdrawing toward its entry zone under no order.` is byte-equal; the dormant row's held order is cleared at the possess (an order given to the mind is not the pilot's and does not outlive the seat); a second `Released` over a hull its mind already flies touches nothing (where the reflex re-adopted — the asymmetry declared, no production path sends it). The stock memory gained its fifteenth key, `ordered` (set by an order's Withdraw, cleared by the dry or hurt edge and by the seed), because the restoration edge — a healthy Withdraw under no order returns to Patrol — had two origins before the seed (an order's expiry and nothing else) and gained a third, and reads `ordered` now, so a released hull is left to Q.7's rally as the reflex was; `standard.rhai` carries the same key and `STANDARD_SHA256` moved with it (`8b3ec78c…` → `102cc7d2…`), the mind pins' second declared move. **The three readers** read a cached doctrine state, `MindState.doctrine_state` — `StockMemory.state` as the row's last landed memory decodes it, `Startup` for an empty, absent or ill-shaped one; never hashed (the bytes it is derived from are), never carried (`SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` 8 stays; re-derived at decode), refreshed at every write of the memory, read directly (`any_mind_approaching`) by the tempo and through `Arena::machine_state` by the squad brief and the catch-up (`Disabled` for a wreck or a disabled hull under either machine, the reflex row's state under `Controller::Reflex`, `None` for a pilot's hull, a dormant row never read): the tempo's `ai_approaching` raises `Alert` for a mind in `Approach` one substep after the reflex's tick would have (the landing); the squad brief's `doing:` histogram and `member N: state …` read a mind member's state where M1a.5's placeholder read `Startup` (D-UM-49 kept: a state word, never the memory); the Y.4 catch-up's machine words (`MachineWords::of_mind`) read the state, the mind's `FrameOrder` objective and its motor goal. **The watch's one machine flag**: `m` for every `is_machine()` hull, `n` retired — the renderers never knew `n`, so a mind read `pilot` until this cut. **The stock source with no store**: `minds::source_lookup` answers the compiled-in Standard Doctrine for its own digest before the store at both refusals (`resume::refuse_unrunnable_seals` and `arena_bridge::sealed_sources`), `standard_digest` computed once a process, and `ResumeRefusal::MindSource` reads `{unit}'s mind source {digest} is neither this server's Standard Doctrine nor in its store`; a live record stripped of its seals (the pre-M3.3 shape) is now voided by that installer's name, its snapshot carrying scripted seal references no record names a source for (*since the fill decision, the next entry, only when the snapshot's references are `scripted`: a production snapshot's are `native` and such a record resumes*). **The fourteen divergences** of T1 from the reflex that §17 asks this document to hold (M1a.6's thirteen counters and the uncounted fourteenth; at this commit, over the pooled lane's thirty matches: tiebreak=80 line=0 support=0 reads_dead=0 landing=10562 no_line=0 fan=11 order_hint=0 order_end=0 range_round=23 plot_cell=16 deg_round=0 fallback=2 cond_fire=2811 cond_stale=9 unwarranted=0 unlawful=0 floor_held=0 slot_starved=0 slot_parallel=449): `tiebreak` (equal utilities broken by contact id, not roster order), `line` (the plot's own cell when no line), `support` (the self-charge on a mind), `reads_dead` (one read-dead rule for every slot target), `landing` (every think acts one substep after it was taken; the dominant one), `no_line`, `fan`, `order_hint` (the classified re-key), `order_end`, `range_round`, `plot_cell`, `deg_round`, `fallback`; and (14) a turret-killed T1 hull reads unarmed and patrols where T0 withdraws. Beside them M1b.2's seven (`cond_fire`, `cond_stale`, `unwarranted`, `unlawful`, `floor_held`, `slot_starved`, `slot_parallel` — the second D-UM-43 exception, named, counted, not retuned, a lead ruling owed: T1 30.491 shots a minute against T0's 24.977), the uncounted `hold_after_end` (Q-M7: production behaviour for the fill from this cut) and the three residuals (no authored speed, no cost row, no match mode). Doc 17 §24's "fourteen" are these. **The counted coverage at the flip commit** (Gate B, `cargo test --release -p tacsim --test twin`, byte-identical to `M1B5_POOLED_RECORD` — the twin bypasses `start`, seats nobody and keeps its fixture terms): `twin_pooled: n=30 t1_wins=15 t0_wins=15 draws=0 interval=[0.332, 0.668] decisive_interval=[0.332, 0.668] control_draws=2 control_red_wins=26 control_blue_wins=2` · `twin_divergences: tiebreak=80 line=0 support=0 reads_dead=0 landing=10562 no_line=0 fan=11 order_hint=0 order_end=0 range_round=23 plot_cell=16 deg_round=0 fallback=2 cond_fire=2811 cond_stale=9 unwarranted=0 unlawful=0 floor_held=0 slot_starved=0 slot_parallel=449 first_divergence_ms=101377` · `twin_divergence_basis: thinks=10562 ordered_thinks=0 (support, order_hint, order_end and deg_round read only under an order; the pool orders nothing)` · `twin_gate_b: side=t1 shots=2898 shots_per_min=30.491 time_to_first_shot_ms=113627 first_shots=30 states_at_end=[startup=0,patrol=3,approach=0,engage=26,withdraw=0,disabled=31] wrecked=31 cond_shots=2811 parallel=449` · `twin_gate_b: side=t0 shots=2374 shots_per_min=24.977 time_to_first_shot_ms=113377 first_shots=30 states_at_end=[startup=0,patrol=0,approach=0,engage=26,withdraw=10,disabled=24] wrecked=24 cond_shots=0 parallel=0` · `twin_gate_b: side=control shots=4750 shots_per_min=25.217 time_to_first_shot_ms=112377 first_shots=60 states_at_end=[startup=0,patrol=0,approach=0,engage=60,withdraw=8,disabled=52] wrecked=46 cond_shots=0 parallel=0` · `twin_gate_b_pool: path_queries=1135 corner_refusals=0 control_path_queries=1056 control_corner_refusals=0`. **The seed**: `DrawLabel::Mind` is still drawn by nothing on a production path (neither `NativeStockMind` nor `standard.rhai` draws); the production hash moved for the controller byte, the mind rows, the seal digests and the landing timing, never for a draw; an authored mind's draws (M5; *doc 17's M6 since 17b cut M5's record, C-21: M5 fields no authored mind*) are a function of the match seed and its own thinks. **The landing latency**: a mind thinks at its lattice millisecond and acts at the landing one substep later (250 ms on shipped content), its answer written at the head of the next substep (M4.1); the reflex acted at its tick; so an order reaches a mind-flown hull up to 250 ms later than it reached the reflex, and a slot's own shot at `WeaponReady` offsets that for fire. **The one production re-bless**, declared with its reasons (the controller byte folds `2` where it folded `1`; the due loop runs `MindThink`/`MindLand` where it ran `ReflexTick`; hash item 3's reflex rows are gone and item 3c's mind rows fold in their place; the seal digests are the production engine's and every `mind_sealed` line's `source` is the script's digest): `m1a5_the_production_hash_does_not_move` / `m1a1_the_moved_motor_folds_the_same_bytes` `0x18df_dd94_73aa_5a36` → `0x692d_5b0e_3c04_93a6` and `0x5689_35a8_5830_609d` → `0xd243_f6a1_30c0_00c7`, `DUEL_HASH_AT_HEAD` `0xd25d_39ba_9b04_7064` → `0x65eb_692a_b7a8_a72d`, `M1B0_J_LINES` re-recorded (61 lines to 50, every `fired` the mind's or its slot's) and `M1B0_DUEL_LINES` (34 to 34); the (j) field itself took the two-T1 shape of `tests/minds.rs` (every hull seeing ten cells, the seated tank presenting every band), because a mind's slot fires at odds and a default profile presents none — the reflex-era field held a mind in `Approach` with no shot where the reflex fired seven times; the control `M1B0_SEAT_LINES` (two seated pilots, no release) moved only in the `source` field of its two `mind_sealed` lines; the mind-fixture pins (`M1B5_TWO_T1_EVENTS`, the memory digests, the M3.4 fault fixtures, `M2_CROSS_PLATFORM_PINS`) moved once for the script's digest and the fifteenth key. **The fixtures**: `crates/mushd/tests/arena_custody.rs`' custody lens lost its idle device (it pushed the reflex's `decide_ms` past its lifetime; a mind thinks on its class row's grid and reads no cadence knob) and takes a new one from the hulls themselves — the idle hull immobile and dry (its engine destroyed, its magazines emptied; not inert, since an immobile hull with no weapon is §3.8's *disabled with no weapons* and ends the match at 0 ms), the driven hull unarmed (an unarmed T1 patrols and never closes), its drive claim re-aimed at custody's own promise (the pose the world gets back is the arena's own last frame, a cell or more from where the hull went out); the three released-tank fixtures of `tests/reflex.rs` are ported onto the mind now (`t1_a_released_seat_hands_the_tank_to_its_mind_in_withdraw`, `t1_a_released_cold_tank_starts_itself_up_and_stays_in_withdraw`, `t1_a_released_cold_tank_is_one_event_list_and_one_hash_sequence_at_three_cadences`); the rest of `tests/reflex.rs` is M4.4's, every other fixture and the harness lanes M4.5's (`arena.windows.json` unmoved: every lane builds custody on `Controller::Reflex` directly), and `Controller::Reflex`, `ReflexTick` and `SealEngine::Reflex` (decode-only from this cut) are M4.6's deletion. No pack, no proto, no wire or frame change; nothing a live world must take by hand beyond a `mushd.toml` that named `fill_controller`.
- **§6 "The reflex controller" (the AI fill's engine: doc 17 §18's fast path taken), §7 (the seal every hull is checked out under)**: as decided (the fill decision, 2026-10-09, `ad0b8faf`, its own commit after 17b cut M4.5 and before M4.6's code — the sha written in by 17b cut M4.7's record, the entry having ridden the commit it describes; doc 17 §18 "The fast path has one trigger" and §22's M4, which ask for the fast-path decision to be recorded here; doc 17 §24). **The rule** (17b cut M4.2, `crates/tacsim/src/match_/seal.rs`' `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE`): the minds lane's fill variant (thirty-six Class I hulls a side on the constant at the authored reach) over three idle-host weekly runs of `scripts/harness-weekly.sh` on ALIEN18, the owner's host — `held=yes` three times keeps `scripted`, any `held=no` moves the fill to `native`; a `sound=no` run is a lane fault, re-run and never counted. **The owner's three runs** (tree `f19a88fb`), verbatim: run 1 (Fri Oct 9 11:13:58 PDT) `harness_minds_verdict: variant=fill_authored cohort=authored held=no sound=yes assert=true mode=weekly wake_p99_ms=17.297 wake_lens_ms=12 window_p99_ms=17.166 window_lens_ms=20 window_comparable=48 window_p99_other_ms=22.613 faults=0 ops_total=1763485 budget_p1=718 cpu_p99_ms=6.070 cpu_lens_ms=7.995 authored_at_class_at_end=65 failures=1 | per-wake p99 17.297 ms is past the 12 ms lens | unsound=0 | none`; run 2 (11:19:11 PDT) `harness_minds_verdict: variant=fill_authored cohort=authored held=no sound=yes assert=true mode=weekly wake_p99_ms=20.750 wake_lens_ms=12 window_p99_ms=20.750 window_lens_ms=20 window_comparable=48 window_p99_other_ms=25.640 faults=0 ops_total=1763485 budget_p1=718 cpu_p99_ms=6.395 cpu_lens_ms=10.026 authored_at_class_at_end=65 failures=2 | per-wake p99 20.750 ms is past the 12 ms lens; per-window p99 20.750 ms over the 48 comparable windows (the lattice's substeps, inline think time inside Wake.ms) is past the 20 ms lens of the authored cohort | unsound=0 | none`; run 3 (11:23:03 PDT) `harness_minds_verdict: variant=fill_authored cohort=authored held=no sound=yes assert=true mode=weekly wake_p99_ms=17.817 wake_lens_ms=12 window_p99_ms=17.817 window_lens_ms=20 window_comparable=48 window_p99_other_ms=22.693 faults=0 ops_total=1763485 budget_p1=718 cpu_p99_ms=5.980 cpu_lens_ms=9.010 authored_at_class_at_end=65 failures=1 | per-wake p99 17.817 ms is past the 12 ms lens | unsound=0 | none`. **The outcome**: all three counted, all `held=no` — `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE = native`. The AI fill and every seated hull are sealed with Standard Doctrine on the native stock mind (doc 17's T1) at `[tacsim.minds].fill_class`, so a release hands any hull to a native mind; the Rhai Standard Doctrine (`standard.rhai`) stays the exact reference, held equal to T1 by Gate A and the twin (no divergence between the runners), and a scripted seal still runs it (a fixture's, or a match checked out before the decision, which resumed on the runner its seal names, the stock source being compiled in, until 17b cut M4.6's format 9, which voids every older snapshot). **What a pilot reads is unchanged**: the `mind_sealed` line names no engine, nothing user-facing prints one, and the shipped fixtures' tapes are byte-equal but for their substep hashes. **The production hash moved for the seal digests alone** (a seal's engine is folded into its digest and the digest into the mind row's hash since 17b cut M3.6): `m1a5_the_production_hash_does_not_move` / `m1a1_the_moved_motor_folds_the_same_bytes` `0x692d_5b0e_3c04_93a6` → `0xc734_ca3c_2cce_2a0c` and `0xd243_f6a1_30c0_00c7` → `0xbabf_b4c3_71e8_786d` (the substep count, the thinks and the faults unmoved), `DUEL_HASH_AT_HEAD` `0x65eb_692a_b7a8_a72d` → `0x999b_45cb_390f_4af8`, and `M1B0_J_LINES` (50 lines) and `M1B0_DUEL_LINES` (34) re-recorded with every byte but `hash` equal. **Context, not counted**: the VPS conclav.io (linux, the production host; 4 vCPU) ran the same tree three times the same day, `held=no` on every lens including the CPU lens (fill wake p99 35.341, 37.587 and 38.223 ms; CPU p99 15.455, 15.837 and 16.735 ms against computed lenses of 12.564, 12.690 and 13.326); each host's no-jitter diagnostic (`TACSIM_MINDS_JITTER_PER_MILLE=0`, never a decision run) read ALIEN18 `held=yes` (wake p99 9.946 ms) and the VPS `held=no` (wake p99 19.023, window p99 35.961); `ops_total=1763485` on all eight lines, so the interpreted work is the same on Windows and Linux. On the VPS the `stock_ci` and `stock_authored` lines (every hull native) read `held=no` too, on all three runs (`stock_ci` wake p99 17.027–24.306 ms, window 17.206–29.971; `stock_authored` wake 28.945–35.872, window 29.153–38.975; minds CPU p99 only 1.852–4.559) and on the diagnostic (`stock_ci` wake 12.238, window 24.641; `stock_authored` wake 16.776, window 32.247), and the minds-free arena lanes miss the 12 ms lenses on the same runs (authored engaged p99 14.908–16.328; CI window p99 14.807–19.989): on that host at 300 hulls the arena's own step dominates, and native saves some 13 ms of minds CPU per window there (the fill's CPU p99 15.455–16.735 against `stock_authored`'s 2.460–2.962) without bringing it under the lenses — a capacity finding recorded for the owner, not this decision's to fix. **Cross-host determinism**: the delta against `arena.linux.json` (blessed at `53457605`) read the deterministic block equal (106 fields) and all four minds blocks equal (`fill_authored` 102 fields, `stock_ci` 30, `stock_authored` 30, `ceiling_authored` 34) on the VPS (linux/4/srv1930616, rustc 1.98.0) and on ALIEN18 (windows/32, against a temporary copy of the file as `arena.windows.json`, removed afterwards), timing skipped (host differs). The harness's `minds.fill_authored` block is re-blessed for the native fill: against the M4.5 baseline the delta (this container, release, no bless) read the deterministic block equal (106 fields), `minds.stock_ci`, `minds.stock_authored` and `minds.ceiling_authored` equal (30, 30, 34), and `minds.fill_authored` different in seven leaves and no other — `engine` `"scripted"` → `"native"`, `ops_total` 1,763,485 → 0, `fees_total` 110,647 → 0, `ops_per_window_peak` 51,626 → 0, `ops_per_substep_peak` 18,840 → 0, `rhai_thinks` 2,009 → 0 and `final_substep_hash` 14533477979303065489 → 10681623480133624222 — its thinks (8,751), landings (8,758), faults (0) and condition fires (2,468) unmoved, and the 2,009 Class I thinks that ran in Rhai now run natively (`native_minded_thinks=2009`, the lane's new integrity count: a native-sealed variant is `sound=` only if its slots thought above the ROM and never in Rhai). The variant's `held=` word is no longer a decision input but a capacity reading for the owner from here, and it is still asserted under `TACSIM_ASSERT` as every variant's is: on a weekly run (`scripts/harness-weekly.sh` exports it) a fill `held=no` fails the minds lane and so blocks that run's bless. No pack, no proto, no wire, frame or snapshot format change.
- **§3.3 and the O.3 entry (a range target powers up, then parks), §6 "Deliberation" and the Z.2 entry (a `SUPPORT` whose charge leaves the match ends), and the machine behaviour no mind has yet**: as built (17b cut M4.6b, 2026-10-10, `3a3c469d`, under the owner's rulings of 2026-10-09; doc 17 §24 holds each ruling, each loss and its mirror). A hull authored with no speed reads immobile on its own mind's view once its power is online, so `SIM RANGE`'s `target_mk1` starts up (a thermal sight reads it hot), then parks `Disabled` and gives no intent — from 17b cut M4.3 to here it started up, searched and turned in place; the pilot's frame and the keyframe are untouched. A mind's `Support` whose charge has left the match ends at the supporter's next landing with one `order_expired` line of the reflex's shape (but for STOP E's two residuals, doc 17 §24); a charge its supporters see destroyed ends nothing on the row (the mind stops obeying, the order stands to its deadline). Carried to M5, and so done by no machine since 17b cut M4.3: the O.1 and AE.3 entries' machines in a Hold match (driving to the spot, holding the square, a released drone driving there), the Z.2 entry's supporter firing first on what its charge is fighting, the I.3 entry's placed machine searching anew from where staff put it (a placed mind keeps its goal and stands until it next changes), the AE.2 entry's guard that leaves a machine untaught by a blocked round fired before it climbed (a mind is taught `no line`), the E.4 entry's re-plan of a machine whose round the ground stopped (`T-42`), the AI.1 entry's hunt fan within a hunter's own memory, and a wreck's motor row forgotten. `docs/help` keeps its Hold, support and support-end sentences, and the `SUPPORT` acknowledgement its "fires first on what … is fighting", as written (the owner: M5 restores).
- **§6 "The reflex controller" (deleted), §3.7 `ORDER` (`NotMachine`), §4 and §3.1 "Durability" (`SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` 9), §3.1 "Pulse phases" step 5 and the Z.2 entry (the order's issuer leaves the hash)**: as built (17b cut M4.6c, 2026-10-10, `b91dea0a`, under the owner's rulings of 2026-10-09; doc 17 §24 holds the detail). The reflex controller is deleted — `arena/reflex.rs`, `Controller::Reflex` (hash code 1, retired and never reused), `DueKind::ReflexTick`, the reflex rows and hash item 3 — so every machine is a unit mind (the AI fill a native one since the fill decision's entry above) and §6's reflex paragraphs describe the doctrine Standard Doctrine re-expresses; `[tacsim.arena.reflex]` and `ReflexConfig` keep their names, `decide_ms` the motor's tick. `ORDER` naming a hull that is not a machine's is refused `NotMachine` (word `not_machine`, where it was `not_reflex`), the cockpit's sentence ("That tank has a pilot in it.") unchanged and no tape carrying the word. An order's issuer, which the Z.2 entry's hash folded, is now a field beside a mind's order, written at its landing and cleared with it, carried by the snapshot and never hashed (the owner's ruling; the hint is re-keyed once, at the landing, in the issuer's scope). `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` 8 → 9 (the reflex section out; the issuer and the seal's Standard Doctrine bit in): the first reload onto this build voids every match running across it `aborted(restart)` ("the snapshot does not decode: snapshot format 8 is not 9"), its tanks restored from check-out; no frame field, wire key, pilot sentence or TOML key moved. A mind not sealed on Standard Doctrine reads `unknown` to the leader's brief and the catch-up, and never counts as approaching for the tempo — no production seal is one before M5.
- **§3.7 the lobby verbs (`SIM MIND`, `SIM AI MIND`), §3.8 (the pool's sentence; seated hulls at the pool) and §7 (`[tacsim.minds].classes`)**: as built (17b cut M5.1, 2026-10-11, `4078b9dd`, on the owner's M5 rulings of 2026-10-10; doc 17 §24 holds the detail). `SIM MIND` (the fourteenth `SIM` selector) and `SIM AI MIND` (read inside `SIM AI`, before its number) choose a hull's mind during `Selection` against a holdings seam (`match_::holdings`; in production Standard Doctrine and its six presets for every character, keyed by the character who boards — doc 17's Q-UM-13, confirmed by the owner), each reply to the chooser alone; the choice is fitted at once (over-class on a governed core, the profile count at the class the mind will seal at, the card's pair on every chassis and the odds floor on an armed one, then the pool at the hull's own class), `SIM UNIT` re-fits a standing choice, and the board names a slot's mind to its own character and staff and reads `, a mind` for everyone else, in `Selection` only (a side's fill row likewise for its lead). **The pool's wording** (the owner's OQ-11): the refusal reads `This tank would think {would} ops a second; its side's half of the centre's pool is {half} and {spoken} are spoken for.` (it read `This side would think …; its half of the centre's pool is …`), `StartError::Pool` names its hull in the long form (`{callsign}: …`, logged at `warn`), and the last pilot to `BOARD` reads the call-off's short form (a fixture's: `The match could not start: red's compute (3,000 ops a second) is over its half (1,904).`). **Seated hulls at the pool** (the M4.3 entry's "M5's gap"): every hull that chose no mind is admitted at `[tacsim.minds].fill_class`, seated or not, and a chosen hull at the class its mind will seal at; a change that lowers a slot's load is never refused, one that keeps or raises it is asked of the side after it; none can refuse at the stock constants. `[tacsim.minds].classes.*.rows` is validated 2 to 32 (it was 1 to 32), so every class holds the seat's two rows. No hash, format, frame, seal byte or tape line moved; the check-out still sealed Standard Doctrine on Line until the next entry.
- **§3.8 (the seal: the chosen mind, its profiles, the unarmed hull and the floor, over-class), §4 and §3.1 "Durability" (`SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` 10), §3.7 (a seat's profile switch) and the M4.6b entry (STOP E's charge)**: as built (17b cut M5.2, 2026-10-11, `73f85147`, with the orchestrator's S1 and D21 rulings of that day; doc 17 §24 holds the detail). The check-out seals each hull from its slot's or its side's fill choice — the held mind's identity, rating and engine and up to the sealed class's profiles (Class I keeps two), a chosen scripted source installed in the store's memory before its seal is used and a scripted seal whose source the store lacks refused before any write — and every hull that chose nothing exactly as before: `MindSeal.profiles` (each profile's name and card digest) is written last and skipped when the set is the Line card alone, so every default seal digests as at `5f505c8` (pinned) and no production hash pin moved. The seal line `Sealed for this sortie: Standard Doctrine (Sniper, Line), Class I; MIND reads it.` is said before `Good hunting.` to each seated pilot whose slot chose a mind (or whose choice was substituted), and to nobody else. **The unarmed hull and the floor** (doc 17's C-23): the seal, as the choice since M5.1, runs the card's helm pair on every hull and the Compact floor's probe only on a hull with a weapons station, so an unarmed hull under a raised `odds_floor_permille` no longer calls a match off `CardUnlawful`; what it then flies is doc 17's Q-UM-12 default (Standard Doctrine with its fire half absent), relied on and unruled. **Over-class** (doc 17's C-24 as amended by the S1 ruling: a governed core at Class 0 flies the ROM, as no core does): a mind rated above a governed core of Class I or more is refused `StartError::MindOverClass` by hull — fixture-only, Standard Doctrine being rated I — and a card the seal refuses is called off `StartError::CardRefused` by hull, naming no profile. **A seat's profile switch**: `UnitState.card_profile` (folded only off its default) and `IntentKind::Profile { index }` on the helm, taken from a seat alone, continuous under its own key (`continuous_code` 5), handled in its own arm before the power and motion gates (a cold or immobile hull switches), refused `Refusal::Profile` (word `profile`) for no seal, an index past the set, the profile already active or a landing inside 10,000 ms of the last switch, journalled as one `mind_card` line; a released hull's new mind row flies the active profile's card, and the `Withdraw` seed is written only over a Standard Doctrine seal. Q-UM-15's substitution (a non-Doctrine choice sealed as Standard Doctrine with the fallback `humans on the other side` when both sides hold a claimed slot, and `Humans on the other side: your fill and any released hull fly Standard Doctrine.` told once to each pilot it touches) is fixture-only. **STOP E's charge** (the M4.6b entry's two residuals closed): `MindState.order_charge`, written from a landed `Support { of }` and cleared with the order, folded inside the order's `Support` arm alone; the arena ends a held `Support` whose charge has left `Arena::units`, so a `Support` landed with `of: None` and a supporter blind to its charge for a contact TTL now end when the charge leaves (`help cockpit`'s support-end sentence is true of a charge that leaves; a charge seen destroyed still ends nothing on the row). **The format move**: `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` 9 → 10 (`SealRef.profiles`, `UnitState.card_profile`, `MindState.order_charge`, the two new variants), the `af1` shape pin re-blessed once (`202e7d4f…`, 509 lines → `811a1ce7…`, 556), its walk now holding a switched profile, a motor row's inner shapes and every objective's fields; the first reload onto a build of this cut voids every match running across it `aborted(restart)` ("snapshot format 9 is not 10"), its tanks restored from check-out, nothing migrating; `LIVE_FORMAT` stays 1, every live-record addition of the cut being `#[serde(default)]`. `STANDARD_SHA256`, `MIND_ABI`, `API_VERSION` and `FRAME_VERSION` unmoved.
- **§3.7 `BOARD` and `EXIT`, §3.8 (Disconnects) and the Y.3 entry (the board's drop word, the reconnect), the Z.2 entry (the release, the support acknowledgement, the debrief's release line), §6 "Provider failure" (the lapse), §5 (the catch-up's `unknown`) and the help (what the machines do)**: as built (17b cut M5.3, 2026-10-11, `fb1c4691`, and 17b cut M5.6, 2026-10-11, `46f6a796`; doc 17 §24). One word for every mind, so no sentence names or marks the kind of a released hull's mind: the board's drop clause is `, flown by its mind` (it was `, flown by doctrine`, as wide; an `EXIT` keeps `, left`, doc 17's C-11), the reconnect line `<tank> is flying on its mind; BOARD takes it back.`, the release acknowledgement `Released red-1 to its mind: it fights on its own until it is ordered again.` (`Released red-1 and red-2 to their minds: they fight on their own until they are ordered again.` for several), and the catch-up's machine state `unknown` (a mind sealed on anything but Standard Doctrine, fixture-only) `The machine was flying on its own mind under no order.` on line mode, the TUI and the web (the wire word stays `unknown`); since M5.6 the order lapse `Squad red-1: your hold order has run out; back on their minds.`, `ORDER`'s help `RELEASE hands them back to their own minds` and the debrief's `released red-1 to their minds.`. The help says what the machines do now (the owner's OQ-6: the text in M5, the behaviour in the residues cut after it): `help simulator`'s Hold paragraph (no machine drives for the key spot; the machines fight what they find), the `SUPPORT` acknowledgement `Ordered red-1 to support RA: it keeps within 150 m of RA and fights what it finds there.` and `help cockpit`'s support paragraph (STOP D's designation half recorded as accepted under doc 17's D-UM-15), `drone_mk1`'s note (the pack at version 3), and the leader's system prompt. The bot's readers moved once (`Standing::Mind`, `Line::OnItsMind`; the lapse, old tail and new, stays a neutral line to it), so the bot deploys from the server's commit. No hash, format or frame moved.
- **§3.7 the cockpit verbs (`MIND`), §5 (the keyframe's `mind` key and the TUI and web block), §3.6 and the V.3 entry (the report's `Your mind` block; the sixth hint)**: as built (17b cut M5.4, 2026-10-11, `51fd6ceb`; 17b cut M5.5, `354e0ae8`; 17b cut M5.6, `46f6a796`; doc 17 §24). `MIND` is the seventeenth cockpit selector (nineteen verbs with `BOARD` and `EXIT`; `m`, `mi` and `min` now resolve to it, no kernel verb losing a prefix), its row in §3.7's table; `Frame.mind` (the sealed set, the active profile, the mind's and the core's classes read live, the next switch, a drop) is compared by `Frame::same_as`, so a switch on a quiet hull sends one frame, and goes out as the seated pilot's keyframe key `mind` (`null` with no seal; never on the replay or the watch; never in a mind's view) with `FRAME_VERSION` held at 3 — a field for the person in the seat is not a version; the fan-out lane's largest panel moved 7,931 → 8,093 bytes against its 16,384. The TUI and the web draw the block as the weapons pane's tail — four rows under the weapon rows (`mind Standard Doctrine · Class I` / `> Sniper   Line` / `dormant while you fly` / `you: helm sensors fire`), in `(no weapon)`'s place on an unarmed hull — so no pane is added and the contacts budget (16b cut AB.1) is untouched, the tail clipped from the bottom and never the weapon rows (the canonical frame loses its channel row at 120 × 30); on the web each sealed profile the mind is not flying prefills `MIND PROFILE <name>` and sends nothing until Enter. `SIM REPORT` carries the pilot's own `Your mind` block (the mind and profiles as sealed, their switches, when it flew the tank, its shots, a drop to the ROM by its cause word), printed only when the slot chose a mind or the mind flew the hull and read by nobody else; its span of when the mind flew is the one report fact not told from the tape or the check-out (V.3's rule, declared), timed on the bridge mirror's `MatchMirror::now_ms`. The sixth first-time hint `Hint: SIM MIND Standard lets your tank's core fly the Registry's doctrine your way when you climb out.` is said at a match's end; `help minds` is new. No hash or format moved.
- **Before IC play** (additions, not changes): per-pulse state durability on an arena writer lane with
  disjoint key ownership and resume from the snapshot are built (16b cut AF, the AF.1 to AF.3 entries
  above; the ledger tail is abandoned behind a marker rather than replayed, 16c Q40 amended); the 10 km
  arena remains. AI takeover of dropped human slots is already built (N.1 and Q.7). An IC sortie's
  durability is its host's, not the arena's: doc 23 D-23-6 puts the custody half of IC play on the
  IC-clock pull ticks (the surface excursion, the port voyage) and leaves the arena, promoted onto a
  site's ground (doc 20 §17), as the fight's host inside a hull's open sortie.
- **§5 (the cockpit's surfaces)**: as built (doc 21 cut G.1a, 2026-10-22; the pixel spike of
  2026-10-21 promoted): the cockpit has a fourth surface beside line mode, the TUI's screen 0 and the
  web's cockpit tab — the **picture**, `Ctrl-G` in `@tui` on a terminal whose probe answered the kitty
  graphics protocol (doc 21 §4.1). It is a first-person raycast of `arena.map` from the frame's own
  `x_cm`, `y_cm` and `heading_mdeg`, the frame's contacts drawn as billboards where the pilot's scope
  plots them (red hostile, blue friendly, khaki unclassified, brown `reads_dead`), with the instruments
  drawn into the picture: a minimap with the named spots, heat, the weapons with their cycles, the
  armour doll, a status strip. It reads those two feeds and nothing else, rendered in the gateway half
  per connection (D-21-1), so §5's rule holds for pixels: a unit the pilot's scope does not hold is not
  drawn. Its own turn keys move a look, not the tank, and the line over the picture prints the look
  against the sim's heading until doc 21 G.4 makes a turn a helm order. No feed, frame or version moved;
  `help cockpit` gains "The picture".
- **World Generator v2 (WG.1–WG.6, doc 30).** The ground becomes content (`D-30-1`) authored through
  `ground.draft.v1` (`tacsim::ground`, `ground-pack`, Web Terrain & Arena Studio, and
  `scripts/ground-extract/`). `RegionDef` and `RegionRec` gain `biome` (`temperate | mars | lunar | ice`,
  `D-30-5`); `HeightfieldDef` carries optional RLE `classes` (`D-30-2`) so quadrant heightfield entries stay
  under `MAX_ENTRY_BYTES` without sparse `tile_patch` pairs (`proving_ground` bumps to atlas v3 / arena v5,
  Byte-exact round-tripped through `proving_ground.draft.json`, `D-30-4`); and the second shipped arena
  `tacsim.arena.winter_valley` (`winter_valley.json`) ships beside the two-half pin (WG.5). In WG.6
  (`D-30-7`, `Q-30-6`), `ArenaDef` accepts up to `MAX_ARENA_SIDE = 512` (sixteen 128-cell quadrants,
  `MAX_ARENA_TILES = 16`), and `arena.map` gains an optional `window: [x0, y0, w, h]` request parameter and
  carries `"origin_cell": [x0, y0]` and `"extent": [full_cols, full_rows]` on the wire so any 512×512 or
  steep 256×256 ground stays within `MAP_MAX_BYTES = 262_144`.

---

## 12. Decisions (owner, 2026-09-16, binding)

| # | Decision | Source |
|---|---|---|
| D-16-1 | One arena task per map; the arena is the single writer for its facts while a match runs; the seam is the eight messages of §3.1 (a ninth, the durability `Snapshot`, since 16b cut AF.2; the AF.2 entry in §11); never a shared lock | 16a Q1 B, review |
| D-16-2 | Adaptive tempo per arena: `Quiet` 5,000 ms, `Alert` 1,000 ms, `Engaged` 500 ms; the model is rate-independent through substeps and timestamped events (amended at 16b cut M.2, 16c Q23: a pilot's discrete action resolves at the end of the pulse that accepted it, everything it schedules from there being a due event; the M.2 entry in §11) | 16a Q2 B |
| D-16-3 | OOC matches hold state in RAM and the arena never touches the store; the ledger is streamed through the world task; a crash voids the match; per-pulse durability and resume are requirements before IC play (amended at 16b cut AF, 16c Q40: the arena is snapshotted every `snapshot_ms` of arena time and the world task writes the document on its own lane beside its live record; a server restart resumes the match from the last snapshot, the tail abandoned behind a marker, and voids it by name only when it cannot; the AF.1 to AF.3 entries in §11); and, gained at 17b cut M5 by doc 17's D-UM-50 (the first cut that reads an IC holding from the sim), an OOC match posts nothing to, consumes nothing of and reads nothing from the IC world but D-UM-49's mind holdings | 16a Q3 C, Q4 A, review; doc 17 D-UM-50 |
| D-16-4 | Integer authoritative state; integer `Pose`; draws derived from `(seed, label)`; a state hash per substep | 16a Q5 A, Q10 B, review |
| D-16-5 | One 2.56 km arena at 10 m cells; the 10 km path is an addition (amended 2026-10-30: the sim's window is 256 a side by constant and no cut has changed it; doc 30 Q-30-6 allows authored maps of 512 a side, played through the frame window of doc 30 D-30-7, cut WG.6, not yet built; doc 32 Q-CF-51 makes the side a per-region value with 256 the shipped default; the 10 km path is unchanged; the entry in §11) | 16a Q11 A; doc 30 Q-30-6; doc 32 Q-CF-51 |
| D-16-6 | M32 is the unit truth; persistent per-pod tanks reset by plan; a wreck is a named condition plus revoked capabilities; hit location by facet table | 16a Q14 A, Q15 A, review |
| D-16-7 | Kinematic motion in one-cell substeps; a uniform-grid spatial index | 16a Q13 A, Q16 A, review |
| D-16-8 | Scheduled impacts; per-mille resolution from dispersion against silhouette with cover, motion and gunnery terms; fixed damage per ammunition; bursts as one binomial draw; the percentage is shown; a blocked shot costs like any shot | 16a Q17 A, Q18 A, Q33, review |
| D-16-9 | Armour to floor, overflow to internals, section consequences from a closed vocabulary; disabled precedes destroyed | 16a Q19 A, Q20 A |
| D-16-10 | Detection is range plus LOS plus signature with a classification delay; arena contacts are an in-RAM shadow with their own id space; the arena's domain is excluded from every world view; ECM and ECCM are null fields today | 16a Q21 to Q24, review |
| D-16-11 | Pods are interiors; stations on the tank; F3 seats and focus; bare cockpit verbs and `BOARD`; a ten-second startup | 16a Q25 to Q27, review |
| D-16-12 | The minimal lifecycle with a named `Boarding → Running` trigger and `Aborted | Void` from every allocated state; doc 07 §1 with the idle substitution | 16a Q28, Q29, review |
| D-16-13 | `crates/tacsim` is the game and the composition root; definitions through the C1 registry; the closed vocabularies stay Rust; configuration plumbed end to end | 16a Q29 to Q32, review |
| D-16-14 | The character rulesets are deleted first; `mush-ruleset` stays as the sheet library with one game sheet | 16a Q34 A, Q35 A |
| D-16-15 | The story test and the harness are the acceptances; gates are never left red; the cost run is owner-directed | 16a Q7 to Q9, Q36 |
| D-16-16 | Reflex controller in the game crate; bounded deliberation through `LlmService` per squad (N.2 amendment); provider failure is invisible to the pulse | 16a Q37 to Q39 |
| D-16-17 | W1 to W16 are ratified world facts | 16a §9 |
| D-16-18 | Extension order: lasers, bipeds, thermal, ECM, ECCM, elevation | 16a Q40 |
| D-16-19 | The harness follows D-H-8 in CI (report-only, on demand and scheduled) and asserts under `TACSIM_ASSERT=1` locally or on a dedicated runner; an owner wish to gate CI on it supersedes D-H-8 and is recorded here if given | review |
| D-16-20 | The shipped `mush-sim` combat verb lane is retired; the arena is the only combat lane | review |
